# Summary

- Implemented a dropdown menu for the user icon in the navbar, including profile and logout options.
- Fixed the logout button to use an icon instead of text for a cleaner look.
- Added a toast notification for login errors, which appears automatically if an error attribute is present.

# Log

- **Navbar User Menu**: Added dropdown with "Thông tin chung" and "Đăng xuất".
- **Dropdown Issue**: Provided troubleshooting steps for when the dropdown is not visible (login state, cache, JS).
- **Logout Button**: Changed "Đăng xuất" text to a logout icon.
- **Login Error Toast**: Implemented a toast that shows on login error, using the error attribute.

{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"2ac48bc9-192a-45d8-af09-26e8a4cdc437","content":"I’ve confirmed the login page uses `param.error`; I’m now adding a real toast that triggers automatically from that error message so users see it immediately on load.","toolRequests":[{"toolCallId":"call_QgjDTjePuryFMdAMjzeyG97w","name":"apply_patch","arguments":"{\"explanation\":\"Add a login error toast driven by param.error and show it automatically on page load.\",\"input\":\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Update File: d:\\\\project-coding-files\\\\GitHub\\\\LegendCoffee\\\\src\\\\main\\\\resources\\\\templates\\\\authen\\\\login.html\\n@@\\n-                    <form class=\\\"space-y-6\\\" method=\\\"POST\\\" th:action=\\\"@{/login}\\\">\\n-                        <div th:if=\\\"${param.error}\\\" class=\\\"bg-error/10 border-l-4 border-error p-3 mb-4\\\">\\n-                            <p class=\\\"text-xs text-error font-bold\\\"\\n-                                th:text=\\\"${param.error[0] == 'auth_failed' ? 'Email hoặc mật khẩu không chính xác.' : 'Có lỗi xảy ra, vui lòng thử lại.'}\\\">\\n-                            </p>\\n-                        </div>\\n+                    <form class=\\\"space-y-6\\\" method=\\\"POST\\\" th:action=\\\"@{/login}\\\">\\n+                        <div id=\\\"login-error-message\\\" th:if=\\\"${param.error}\\\" class=\\\"hidden\\\"\\n+                            th:text=\\\"${param.error[0] == 'auth_failed' ? 'Email hoặc mật khẩu không chính xác.' : 'Có lỗi xảy ra, vui lòng thử lại.'}\\\">\\n+                        </div>\\n@@\\n-    <script>\\n+    <div id=\\\"login-error-toast\\\"\\n+        class=\\\"fixed top-6 right-6 z-[200] max-w-sm bg-error text-on-error px-4 py-3 rounded-lg shadow-xl opacity-0 translate-y-[-8px] pointer-events-none transition-all duration-300\\\"\\n+        role=\\\"alert\\\" aria-live=\\\"polite\\\"></div>\\n+\\n+    <script>\\n@@\\n         function confirmForceChange() {\\n             alert('Đổi mật khẩu thành công!');\\n         }\\n+\\n+        function showLoginErrorToast(message) {\\n+            const toast = document.getElementById('login-error-toast');\\n+            if (!toast || !message) return;\\n+\\n+            toast.textContent = message;\\n+            toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'translate-y-[-8px]', 'pointer-events-none');\\n+\\n+            window.setTimeout(() => {\\n+                toast.classList.add('opacity-0', 'translate-y-[-8px]', 'pointer-events-none');\\n+            }, 3200);\\n+        }\\n+\\n+        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\\n+            const loginErrorEl = document.getElementById('login-error-message');\\n+            if (!loginErrorEl) return;\\n+\\n+            const message = loginErrorEl.textContent ? loginErrorEl.textContent.trim() : '';\\n+            if (message) {\\n+                showLoginErrorToast(message);\\n+            }\\n+        });\\n     </script>\\n*** End Patch\"}","type":"function"}],"reasoningText":""},"id":"e15fd6b9-583a-4e63-8178-ff0836648cb8","timestamp":"2026-04-19T15:49:32.905Z","parentId":"6ac67733-dbf4-4a02-9992-1fcafaccd1e1"}

'Email hoặc mật khẩu không chính xác.' : 'Có lỗi xảy ra, vui lòng thử lại.'}\">\n+                        </div>\n@@\n-    <script>\n+    <div id=\"login-error-toast\"\n+        class=\"fixed top-6 right-6 z-[200] max-w-sm bg-error text-on-error px-4 py-3 rounded-lg shadow-xl opacity-0 translate-y-[-8px] pointer-events-none transition-all duration-300\"\n+        role=\"alert\" aria-live=\"polite\"></div>\n+\n+    <script>\n@@\n         function confirmForceChange() {\n             alert('Đổi mật khẩu thành công!');\n         }\n+\n+        function showLoginErrorToast(message) {\n+            const toast = document.getElementById('login-error-toast');\n+            if (!toast || !message) return;\n+\n+            toast.textContent = message;\n+            toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'translate-y-[-8px]', 'pointer-events-none');\n+\n+            window.setTimeout(() => {\n+                toast.classList.add('opacity-0', 'translate-y-[-8px]', 'pointer-events-none');\n+            }, 3200);\n+        }\n+\n+        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n+            const loginErrorEl = document.getElementById('login-error-message');\n+            if (!loginErrorEl) return;\n+\n+            const message = loginErrorEl.textContent ? loginErrorEl.textContent.trim() : '';\n+            if (message) {\n+                showLoginErrorToast(message);\n+            }\n+        });\n     </script>\n*** End Patch"}},"id":"62bd1bea-fdc5-414b-a442-1dc71d49dbcd","timestamp":"2026-04-19T15:49:32.971Z","parentId":"c0abb8ac-9780-4f2e-9e33-9721d1623784"}

{"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_9WPZO883P8uTjLlrQkJrETzR","toolName":"apply_patch","arguments":{"explanation":"Update PaymentController exception handling to add error attribute and redirect to /login as requested.","input":"*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: d:\\project-coding-files\\GitHub\\LegendCoffee\\src\\main\\java\\fpt\\legendcoffee\\controller\\PaymentController.java\n@@\n import org.slf4j.Logger;\n import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;\n import org.springframework.ui.Model;\n+import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;\n import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;\n@@\n     @PostMapping(\"/payment/vnpay/create\")\n     public String createVNPayPayment(@RequestParam Long orderId,\n-            HttpServletRequest request) {\n+            HttpServletRequest request,\n+            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes) {\n         try {\n             String ipAddress = WebUtils.getClientIp(request);\n             String paymentUrl = vnPayApplicationService.createPayment(orderId, ipAddress);\n             log.info(\"[Payment] Redirect sang VNPay - OrderId={}\", orderId);\n             return \"redirect:\" + paymentUrl;\n@@\n         } catch (Exception e) {\n             log.error(\"[Payment] Lỗi không xác định khi tạo payment\", e);\n-            return \"redirect:/payment/result?error=internal_error\";\n+            redirectAttributes.addAttribute(\"error\", e.getMessage());\n+            return \"redirect:/login\";\n         }\n     }\n*** End Patch"}},"id":"c342cafa-6dc5-4653-8d34-7a02f87c3fc2","timestamp":"2026-04-19T15:57:02.691Z","parentId":"c3502e90-36d3-4b4a-aa58-055649a896f4"}

Replaced inline toast block in login with fragment include:\n- [src/main/resources/templates/authen/login.html#L280](src/main/resources/templates/authen/login.html#L280)\n\nHow to reuse elsewhere:\n1. Add this in any template:\n- [src/main/resources/templates/fragments/toast.html](src/main/resources/templates/fragments/toast.html)\n2. Include with:\n- th:replace=\"~{fragments/toast :: errorToast('your-toast-id')}\"\n\nYour existing login JS still works because it still targets the same id: login-error-toast.","toolRequests":[]},"id":"5593df9c-8050-4d07-8899-18a51bbac8f8","timestamp":"2026-04-19T16:10:51.080Z","parentId":"e9824a69-f58e-4fe7-932f-98cb5cf8d073"}

{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"a9f4c827-164f-4d94-a1c5-0647a490eb5c","content":"","toolRequests":[{"toolCallId":"call_RRmqHyWegYZz1LdHMtHceKQ0","name":"apply_patch","arguments":"{\"explanation\":\"Update ArticleController to fetch article from database and pass it into model for dynamic rendering.\",\"input\":\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Update File: d:\\\\project-coding-files\\\\GitHub\\\\LegendCoffee\\\\src\\\\main\\\\java\\\\fpt\\\\legendcoffee\\\\controller\\\\ArticleController.java\\n@@\\n package fpt.legendcoffee.controller;\\n \\n+import fpt.legendcoffee.entity.Article;\\n+import fpt.legendcoffee.repository.ArticleRepository;\\n+import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;\\n import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;\\n import org.springframework.ui.Model;\\n import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;\\n+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;\\n+\\n+import java.util.Optional;\\n \\n @Controller\\n+@RequiredArgsConstructor\\n public class ArticleController {\\n+    private final ArticleRepository articleRepository;\\n+\\n     @GetMapping(\\\"/article\\\")\\n-    public String article(Model model) {\\n+    public String article(@RequestParam(required = false) Long id, Model model) {\\n+        Optional<Article> article = (id != null)\\n+                ? articleRepository.findById(id)\\n+                : articleRepository.findFirstByIsActiveTrueOrderByCreatedAtDesc();\\n+        model.addAttribute(\\\"article\\\", article.orElse(null));\\n         return \\\"articles/articles\\\";\\n     }\\n }\\n*** End Patch\"}","type":"function"}]},"id":"4f91f9b2-5074-4466-805d-909511fc39f6","timestamp":"2026-04-20T01:02:49.076Z","parentId":"96e473a4-967b-4517-a999-2c1e7ad63588"}

{"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_RRmqHyWegYZz1LdHMtHceKQ0","toolName":"apply_patch","arguments":{"explanation":"Update ArticleController to fetch article from database and pass it into model for dynamic rendering.","input":"*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: d:\\project-coding-files\\GitHub\\LegendCoffee\\src\\main\\java\\fpt\\legendcoffee\\controller\\ArticleController.java\n@@\n package fpt.legendcoffee.controller;\n \n+import fpt.legendcoffee.entity.Article;\n+import fpt.legendcoffee.repository.ArticleRepository;\n+import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;\n import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;\n import org.springframework.ui.Model;\n import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;\n+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;\n+\n+import java.util.Optional;\n \n @Controller\n+@RequiredArgsConstructor\n public class ArticleController {\n+    private final ArticleRepository articleRepository;\n+\n     @GetMapping(\"/article\")\n-    public String article(Model model) {\n+    public String article(@RequestParam(required = false) Long id, Model model) {\n+        Optional<Article> article = (id != null)\n+                ? articleRepository.findById(id)\n+                : articleRepository.findFirstByIsActiveTrueOrderByCreatedAtDesc();\n+        model.addAttribute(\"article\", article.orElse(null));\n         return \"articles/articles\";\n     }\n }\n*** End Patch"}},"id":"ab7b42d1-a6c8-4c5c-99e5-3d72a5bf1292","timestamp":"2026-04-20T01:02:49.399Z","parentId":"c2c9b375-cd2a-4140-9dfd-e6bf0e9d4bac"}
